- **yegor-bdd**: Apply Bug Driven Development by shaping work as a neutral complaint: have X, should have Y, with repro or evidence. - **yegor-bdd**: Apply Bug Driven Development. Frame every piece of work as a complaint with shape "have X / should have Y / repro". No feature requests, no suggestions, no questions. The complaint is best expressed as a failing/disabled test that proves the bug. A bug title must be a complaint (names the breakage), not a question or a topic — lint and rewrite weak titles at filing time. A good report is also reproducible, rich (expected vs actual + environment), and carries visible reporter effort. Use when filing, reviewing, or closing issues. Only the reporter closes a ticket. ([avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills](https://github.com/avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yegor-bdd))
- **yegor-builds**: One build can't serve every need — use a tiered set. From Yegor Bugayenko's "Four Builds" — a Fast build (seconds, local, unit-only) for the inner loop, a Cheap build (minutes, on every PR, +integration/style), a Preflight build (the slow pre-merge gate, +mutation/load/security), and a Proper build (full regression, at release). Speed early, thoroughness late. Pairs with the 8+2 CI-maturity ladder, and with trust-based dependency versioning (pin untrusted deps to fixed versions, allow ranges for trusted ones, record the rationale). - **yegor-merge-gate**: The trunk (master/main) is read-only and the author never blesses their own merge. From Yegor Bugayenko — no one merges by hand; an automated gate runs the full suite in a clean environment and merges only if green. The gate is zero-tolerance — any single lint/static-analysis violation fails it (warnings == errors, no allowlist). Never merge into a broken master — when the build is red, the only allowed change is the one that fixes it. Use when deciding a PR is ready, before merging, when CI/master is red, or when an agent is about to declare its own work done. ([avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills](https://github.com/avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yegor-merge-gate))

- **yegor-projections**: Forecast from measured delivery rate, not from a spec-derived guess. From Yegor Bugayenko's "How to Estimate Software Cost" — up-front estimates are dishonest because software has no fully-knowable finish line; instead make projections from observed velocity ("we close ~N tickets/week, ~M remain, so ~M/N weeks") and re-issue them as the rate is re-measured. A projection is a falsifiable forecast with an as-of date, not a promise. Use when asked "how long will this take", when tempted to estimate a whole feature up front, or when re-forecasting a roadmap. ([avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills](https://github.com/avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yegor-projections))
- **yegor-review**: Code-review discipline from Yegor Bugayenko. The reviewer's job is to REJECT, not to bless — burden of proof is on the reviewer. Apply the Four NOs (no fear, no compromise, no bullshit, no offense), find the 3 most critical problems, never run the code (a runtime-only bug is a missing test, file it), and auto-reject any PR that changes production code but ships no test. Use when reviewing a PR/diff, giving or receiving review feedback, or deciding how to respond to a review. ([avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills](https://github.com/avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yegor-review))
- **yegor-simba**: Cap work-in-progress and back every status claim with evidence. From Yegor Bugayenko's SIMBA (Simplified Management By Artifacts) — manage by artifacts each with an owner and a reviewer, enforce hard caps on how many things are in flight at once, and require that every progress claim links to verifiable evidence (a PR, a doc, a file, a passing build). Completion is reported by the reviewer, not the owner. Use when work is sprawling across too many in-flight items, when a status report asserts progress without links, when an agent claims "done" without proof, or during a weekly review. ([avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills](https://github.com/avidrucker/yegor-pm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yegor-simba))
- **yegor-small-repos**: Prefer many small single-purpose repositories over one monorepo. From Yegor Bugayenko's "Monolithic Repositories Are Evil" and "Smaller Repository, Higher Quality" — a repo capped around ~50k lines, one language/technology, one problem scope, builds in under a minute. Small repos get stricter style, deeper tests, better reviews, faster onboarding — and, in the AI era, fit inside an agent's context window. Use when a repo is sprawling across many concerns/languages, when builds crawl, when onboarding (human or agent) is hard, or when deciding whether to split. - **yegor-stuck**: What to do when you're blocked, stuck, or tempted to grind heroically or fabricate a fix. An escalating ladder of honest, principled ways to stop being stuck (block, demand docs, reproduce-as-skipped-test, prove-absent, disable-and-ship) instead of thrashing, plus the No-Obligations rule for stale in-progress work (idle past a threshold → drop or re-scope, don't hold the slot). - **yegor-tickets**: All meaningful project communication lives in the issue tracker. No Slack, DMs, or meetings as the primary channel. Decisions don't exist until written as a ticket comment. - **yegor-tickets**: Apply ticket-as-conversation discipline by making the issue tracker the primary record for project decisions, assumptions, blockers, status changes, and direction changes. - **yegor-unit-tests**: Unit-test quality discipline from Yegor Bugayenko. A checklist of named anti-patterns (Liar, Inspector, Mockery, Happy Path, Giant, Free Ride, ...) plus the positive rules — descriptive names, real assertions, test boundaries not just the happy path, isolation, speed, and fakes over mock frameworks. Also: a test is a warranty on the code (every change ships one), the tests-first / two-PR workflow, promoting private methods to their own testable class, test-layout rules, and shipping fakes as production classes. - **yegor-velocity**: Apply Yegor-style velocity discipline by measuring progress primarily as verified closed tickets per time window, not commits, hours, or lines of code. - **yegor-velocity**: Velocity is closed tickets per week, full stop. Commits don't count. Hours don't count. Lines don't count. Reporter verifies; closure comment names the deliverable. - **yemenjpt-geospatial**: تحليل جغرافي ذكي لليمن. يستخدم بيانات الأقمار الصناعية، مؤشرات الإنترنت، والنشاط الليلي لإنتاج مؤشرات ذكية. ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/osint-investigation/yemenjpt-geospatial))
- **yemenjpt-investigation**: سير عمل التحقيق الصحفي في اليمن. يحلل الأخبار، يكشف التناقضات، يعيد بناء القصص المكسورة، ويطبق محرك الشك. ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/investigative-journalism/yemenjpt-investigation))
- **yemenjpt-monitor**: Journalism and research skill: yemenjpt-monitor ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/investigative-journalism/yemenjpt-monitor))
- **yemenjpt-narrative**: تحليل السرديات الإعلامية في اليمن. يرسم خريطة الروايات المتضاربة، يحلل جينوم الإعلام، ويكشف إعادة التدوير والتضليل. ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yemen-context/yemenjpt-narrative))
- **yemenjpt-osint**: أدوات OSINT لليمن. يدمج مصادر محلية ودولية، يبحث في الشخصيات، ويحلل النطاقات والحسابات. ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/osint-investigation/yemenjpt-osint))

- **yemenjpt-report**: توليد تقارير صحفية جاهزة للنشر من البيانات الخام. تحويل التحقيقات إلى قصص مترابطة. ([YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills](https://github.com/YemenJPT/YemenJPT-AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/editorial/yemenjpt-report))

- **yida-custom-page**: JSX 自定义页面开发规范（React 16 平台 Jsx 组件、export function renderJsx 模式、宜搭 JS API、状态管理与编码约束）。用于 .oyd.jsx / .oyb.jsx / renderJsx / 平台 Jsx 组件页面维护。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-custom-page) · ★234)

- **yida-dashboard**: 经营分析看板/驾驶舱/数字大屏/数据大屏产品化交付，默认用自定义页面承载；用于指标判断、趋势分析或投屏监控，不用于普通待办工作台和办事门户。含真实数据、筛选联动、卡片截图、组织内短链和钉钉待办闭环。常规图表使用 yida-rechart；明确 ECharts、复杂 ECharts option 或维护旧图表时使用 yida-chart。普通统计优先 yida-report。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-dashboard) · ★234)
- **yida-data-card**: 仅在用户明确要求操作宜搭原生数据卡片资源时使用，支持创建、读取、配置保存、发布及投放播报。自定义页面展示卡片、仅修改原生卡片引用外层及泛称卡片、指标卡、看板或播报不触发；已明确的原生卡片操作可继续沿用。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-data-card) · ★234)
- **yida-data-manage-ui**: 宜搭数据管理页美化方法：从用户目标推导线条、容器、页签、操作层级、表头、配色、列强调和布局变化，定位实际 token 与局部 CSS，自由组合而非套固定风格。用于当前数据管理页换风格、突出操作或关键列、修复视觉异常，以及已授权的自定义管理页；保留数据、权限和业务动作。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-data-manage-ui) · ★234)
- **yida-data-management**: 查询和维护宜搭表单、流程、任务和子表数据；data seed form/process 批量写入示例、转换日期、核验并返回实例 ID；支持流程当前审批人统计、任务转交和普通记录删除。历史实例版本升级须用户显式选择，并回读核验版本与字段值。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-data-management) · ★234)
- **yida-data-management**: 宜搭数据管理。表单实例/流程实例/任务中心的查询、新增、更新。表单走 /v1/form/，流程走 /v1/process/，不能混用。 ([ythm/openyida](https://github.com/ythm/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-data-management) · ★1)
- **yida-data-source-connectors**: 基于 JSX 组件 自定义页面设计器 dataSourceMap 专用技能。仅用于维护已存在的 Page dataSource.online / this.dataSourceMap.&lt;name&gt;.load() 形态。`YidaCodeCanvas` 组件没有 dataSourceMap；使用 `YidaCodeCanvas` 组件实现的自定义页面数据接入必须改用 yida-canvas-data-binding / HTTP 数据桥。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-data-source-connectors) · ★234)
- **yida-data-tester**: 本技能用于帮助用户自动化测试宜搭平台的各种功能，包括： - 表单数据提交测试 - 公式计算结果验证 - 字段校验规则测试 - 业务规则自动化测试 - 流程审批流转测试 ([lxsin0327-ai/yida-ai](https://github.com/lxsin0327-ai/yida-ai/tree/HEAD/.trae/skills/yida-data-tester) · 3 copies)
- **yida-datav**: 仅在用户明确要求宜搭原生数字大屏、原生数据大屏或 DataV 大屏时使用。创建、复制和编辑前读取当前应用主题，配置配套的明暗与组件样式；查询模板、组件参数和图层配置，回读状态、改名和显式发布。普通大屏默认使用自定义页面。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-datav) · ★234)
- **yida-db-seq-fix**: PostgreSQL Sequence 自动修复工具。检测并修复宜搭环境检测自动建表时 Sequence 起始值问题，避免主键冲突。当用户提到"Sequence"、"主键冲突"、"自增ID错误"、"db-seq-fix"时触发。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-db-seq-fix) · ★234 · 2 copies)
- **yida-density**: 自定义页面信息密度设计规范。提供紧凑、舒适、宽松三种模式、切换 UI 和响应式降级；平台 JSX 组件页面的 didMount/this.utils.isMobile 仅作存量维护示例。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-density) · ★234)
- **yida-density**: 宜搭自定义页面信息密度设计规范。提供紧凑、舒适、宽松三种密度模式的样式模板，支持密度切换和响应式降级，帮助 AI 生成符合场景需求的页面布局。不适用于：非列表/表格类页面（单卡片、表单提交页无需密度设计），或原生报表页面（密度由报表组件自身控制）。 ([ythm/openyida](https://github.com/ythm/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-density) · ★1)
- **yida-design**: 基于整理后的用户需求，设计宜搭应用和页面的主题、布局与视觉效果，按任务交接主题 CSS、页面设计或 design.md。美化已有资源时，先确认方案并备份，只改 UI，不满意时恢复；不修改业务逻辑，不编写 PRD 或页面源码。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-design) · ★234)
- **yida-design-plan**: Plan 模式的视觉设计分支。基于需求选择视觉方向，再结合业务页面规划维护 build-plan.json 的 visualStyle，供 CLI 派生 design.md。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-design/sub_skill/yida-design-plan) · ★234)
- **yida-dev**: 钉钉宜搭低代码开发助手。用于创建表单和自定义页面、编写 JS 动作面板、使用 JS-API、配置数据源、设计流程自动化。适用于宜搭表单开发、JS 代码调试、API 集成等场景。 ([LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills](https://github.com/LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yida-dev) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **yida-dingtalk-openapi**: 将钉钉开放平台官方服务端 API 转成宜搭 HTTP 连接器动作，并供表单、集成自动化或 YidaCodeCanvas 自定义页面安全调用。负责官方文档核对、DingAuth 账号配置暂停与确定性回读。触发词：钉钉开放平台接口、api.dingtalk.com、x-acs-dingtalk-access-token。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-dingtalk-openapi) · ★234)
- **yida-doc-fetcher-batch**: 批量抓取宜搭文档。使用 Node.js v3 提取逻辑，支持表格解析、URL清理、串行处理。 ([yize/yida-doc-fetcher-batch](https://github.com/yize/yida-doc-fetcher-batch/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **yida-document-markdown**: 获取钉钉文档、钉钉在线文档或知识库文档的完整 Markdown 内容。用户提供文档链接并要求读取、提取、总结或继续处理文档内容时使用。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-document-markdown) · ★234)
- **yida-export-conversation**: 导出 AI 对话记录，生成结构化的 Markdown 文档。支持 Claude Code 自动检测，其他环境通过 --input 手动指定。适用于保存和回顾 AI 编程过程记录。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-export-conversation) · ★234)
- **yida-export-conversation**: 导出 AI 对话记录，生成结构化的 Markdown 文档。支持 Claude Code 自动检测，其他环境通过 --input 手动指定。不适用于：导出宜搭表单数据（应使用 yida-data-management），或导出应用配置（应使用 openyida export-app 命令）。 ([ythm/openyida](https://github.com/ythm/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-export-conversation) · ★1)
- **yida-feedback**: 帮用户描述 OpenYida 使用问题或体验建议，整理脱敏 Issue，并在用户愿意时协助个人 Fork PR。适用于反复报错、技能误用、不会提 issue/PR 或没有账号和源码时；普通宜搭产品 VOC 清单用 yida-voc。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-feedback) · ★234)
- **yida-flash-note-to-prd**: 从已有钉钉闪记、会议记录或需求文档内容中提取产品需求，由当前 Agent 直接生成结构化 PRD，适用于本地、远端和托管宿主。若用户只给 taskUuid，先用 yida-tingji 读取内容，再进入本技能。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-flash-note-to-prd) · ★234)
- **yida-flash-note-to-prd**: 闪记转 PRD。从钉钉闪记（AI 听记）、会议记录、需求文档中提取产品需求，生成结构化的 PRD 文档。支持文本、图片、链接三种输入方式，三层 Prompt 架构确保高质量提取。不适用于：直接创建宜搭应用（PRD 生成后还需 yida-app 流程），或处理非需求类文档（如技术文档、代码文件）。 ([ythm/openyida](https://github.com/ythm/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-flash-note-to-prd) · ★1)
- **yida-flow-node-config**: 配置流程和自动化里的处理步骤，包括审批、办理、条件分支、并行、循环、查改数据和通知。人员、角色、按条件选人、字段权限和变量各在一处说明。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-flow-node-config) · ★234)
- **yida-form-api**: Yida form server-side API — CRUD, batch operations, advanced queries with sub-table support. Use when creating, reading, updating, or deleting form instances via DingTalk OpenAPI. Trigger keywords: 表單, form, CRUD, 查詢, query, 批量, batch, 子表, sub-table, 新增, 更新, 刪除. ([farrrr/dingtalk-openapi-skills](https://github.com/farrrr/dingtalk-openapi-skills/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-form-api) · ★1)
- **yida-form-message**: 为已有普通表单配置消息通知，选择新增、编辑、删除或评论后的发送时机、接收人和消息样式。支持工作通知、详情卡片、图文卡片、评论卡片和群置顶卡片等七种模板；按用户要求执行真实发送测试。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-form-message) · ★234)
- **yida-form-permission**: 查询、新增和修改宜搭表单权限组，配置成员范围、数据范围、操作权限和字段权限；支持差异预览和已有矩阵引用。 ([openyida/openyida](https://github.com/openyida/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-form-permission) · ★234)
- **yida-form-permission**: 宜搭表单权限组管理。查询、新增权限组，配置成员/数据权限/操作权限。不适用于：配置页面公开访问分享（应使用 yida-page-config），或配置流程审批节点的字段权限（应使用 yida-process-rule）。 ([ythm/openyida](https://github.com/ythm/openyida/tree/HEAD/yida-skills/skills/yida-form-permission) · ★1)
